# Additive tuning of 2026-11-06, mcp round 1 variant 4, scenario 5

## Run identity

```yaml
model: claude-sonnet-5
served_version: claude-sonnet-5
tree: sha256:057cf511b2890aed9cfbd04883cd9ceae65434dc68bd53e29d8202435d670834
lock: sha256:826dcf9f83c5d9e7fb557d7a1df9753a09a5c70c645d6c9faf7a575256119178
selection: sha256:d99530b2b639bb6bdd279be2a9356a64ce2a0c8cceca354d56957f198a76a20a
read_set: sha256:2a47d5b0401ca6d3239d352781fe101f4d7ba74c60c6a0dbe460e46ae9703c4d
seed: 0
harness: 0.6.1
tier: additive
arm: scenario-5
at: 2026-11-06
cost_cents: 2022
```

**This line is the `screen 4` stage of round 1 of the MCP layer, in the tuning round of the additive campaign that [#1880](https://github.com/headwater-ai/headwater/issues/1880) asked for.** A screen measures one variant on a small batch, so that the round can rank the variants before it pays for a confirmation. Variant 4 is screened from the pushed branch `tune/mcp-r1-v4`, on `a6c51b36`, the commit of [#1937](https://github.com/headwater-ai/headwater/pull/1937). `tools/probe/try.sh launch` ran it from the one-line spec `additive scenario-5 tuning`. It is the `scenario-5` arm of the additive tier over the 22 probes of the tuning set, at 1 repetition per probe, on `claude-sonnet-5` under Claude Code 2.1.288. The owner accepted the plan of this layer [on the issue](https://github.com/headwater-ai/headwater/issues/1880#issuecomment-6655760192).

**What the variant changes.** Variant 4 combines the three other variants: the `instructions` text, the descriptions that say when to call each tool, and the text format as the default of `check` (`8743f22e`). A second commit, `c34e84d6`, carries the amended `instructions` text of variant 1, which names the usual install locations of the command line, on the owner's ruling ([comment 6676328211](https://github.com/headwater-ai/headwater/issues/1880#issuecomment-6676328211)). Both commits change only `engine/crates/query/src/mcp.rs`, as the plan's candidates require.

**One tree, one pin, one launch.** Every session ran in a fresh copy of a `git archive` of the pinned tree, and the engine of that tree. The pin was `c34e84d6` for the whole batch. The canary of 8 sessions cost 829 cents, and the owner read its records and [passed it](https://github.com/headwater-ai/headwater/issues/1880#issuecomment-6679937906). The `tree` digest in the identity above is the digest the assembly recorded.

**What the recorder counted.** The line holds 22 sessions and cost 2022 cents: the intent hook live in 0, 0 stopped at the turn cap, 0 stopped at the session budget, 48 paths outside the workspace named, 0 sessions uncounted, 0 web-tool calls. 21 of the 22 sessions used the MCP layer. A session the recorder could not count adds nothing to either count, so each count is a floor.

**The result.** Variant 4 passed 18 of 22 graded sessions. The base row compares this measurement with the base, scenario 2, which passed 112 of 154 in [the base](additive-tuning-of-2026-11-02-engine-round-1-best-so-far-scenario-2.md). The difference is +9.1 points, with the interval [-12.2, +22.3]. The interval includes zero, and 22 sessions are enough to rank the variants and not enough to keep one. A screen is never kept, and only a confirmation is (owner, 2026-10-22, #1777).

**Where the variant ranks.** The round ranks its screens on the use of the layer first and on the pass rate second. The best so far used the MCP layer in 101 of 154 sessions, in [the best so far](additive-tuning-of-2026-11-06-mcp-round-1-best-so-far-scenario-5.md). The four screens rank in this order: variant 4 (21 used, 18 passed), variant 1 (20 used, 17 passed), variant 2 (16 used, 18 passed) and variant 3 (14 used, 14 passed). Variant 4 ranks first, and it is the variant that goes on to the confirmation of round 1, at 154 sessions.

**The contract of this batch.** The sessions ran under the recorder contract of spec 15: a `bwrap` sandbox with no network of its own, a configuration directory that holds a copy of the credentials and nothing else, the permission mode `dontAsk`, and `WebSearch` and `WebFetch` denied. The owner approved $1,300.00 for this layer. The driver ran this try with a cap of 15000 cents and a session budget of 300 cents.

**What the batch cost.** The driver charged 2022 cents against its cap. A screen is charged to the tuning ceiling of 100000 cents.

**Where the raw recordings are.** The raw session logs are outside git, under `/mnt/build/hw-1880/mcp-r1-v4/a/sessions/L1-*/raw.jsonl` on the host that ran the batch. Nobody deletes them until the report of [#1779](https://github.com/headwater-ai/headwater/issues/1779) is published.

**Where the other figures are.** The rate and the interval of each probe are in the generated result that reads this transcript.

- probe: "HW-PROBE-a-session-records-a-ruling-that-drops-the-help-screen-banner-as-the-settled-successor-of-the-record-the-task-names"

  calls:
    - tool: "mcp__headwater__route"
      argument: "{\"task\":\"Record a decision ruling that no screen of the CLI prints the masthead banner anymore, color stays as-is, and --no-banner / HEADWATER_NO_BANNER flags stay accepted but are now no-ops. This decision replaces docs/decisions/0045-coloring-the-cli-and-where-the-banner-goes.md.\"}"
